                                                      ABOUT THIS REPORT                                                            Meridian Integrated Report 2018




This integrated report is a review of our financial, economic, social
and environmental performance for the financial year ending
30 June 2018 (FY18).
For FY18 the Meridian Group included the parent company Meridian
Energy Limited and all its operational subsidiaries (note the Group
structure in the financial statements). Flux Federation (the software
division of Powershop New Zealand) was created in 2017 and began



                                                                                                                                   OUR
trading on 1 July 2017.
Throughout the report, non-financial data and commentary pertains
to the Meridian Group as much as possible. References to ‘Meridian’
(the parent company), ‘Powershop New Zealand’, ‘Powershop Australia’
and ‘Flux’ are specific to when only parts of the Group are being
discussed (‘Powershop Australia’ refers to our retailing operations
in Australia; the generation activities in Australia are included in the
Generation section). In both the commentary and data, Dam Safety
Intelligence is included in the parent company and Flux-UK Limited
employees are included in ‘Flux’, unless specifically mentioned.
We’ve taken great care to ensure that all data in this report is as


                                                                                                                        I N T E G R AT E D
accurate as possible, and our policy is to seek assurance of both our
financial and non-financial information.
The financial information in this report has been prepared in accordance
with appropriate standards, details of which can be found on page
87, and has been audited by Deloitte Limited on behalf of the Auditor-
General (see the Independent Auditor’s Report on page 115).
The non-financial information in this report has been prepared in
accordance with the Core requirements of the Global Reporting
Initiative’s Sustainability Reporting Standards (the ‘GRI Standards’)
and has been audited by Deloitte Limited (see the Independent
Accountant’s Assurance Report on page 118).                                                                         The purpose of this integrated report is to
Our Meridian Group Greenhouse Gas Inventory Report (available at

report (see pages 26 and 27).
                                                                                                                   creating financial and non-financial value
                                                                                                                      in the short, medium and long terms.

                                                 REMUNERATION
                                                    REPORT
                                                       OUR APPROACH TO REMUNERATING OUR PEOPLE
                                       Attracting, retaining and motivating talented people across the business,
                                         and rewarding them for delivering desired business performance and
                                               long-term shareholder value, is key to Meridian’s success.




                      Our remuneration philosophy is guided by the principles          SHORT-TERM INCENTIVE (STI). The STI is an at-risk
                      that remuneration will:                                          incentive, which may be offered for a specific year, by
                      •   be clearly aligned with our company values, culture          invitation from the Board. Potential STI payments reflect
                          and strategy                                                 the achievement of predetermined company profit levels
                                                                                       and individual performance objectives aligned to business
                      •   support us to attract, retain and engage employees           strategy and goals, and are wholly discretionary. An STI
                      •   be fair, equitable and flexible                              may be paid subject to a behaviour gate and company
                                                                                       financial performance hurdles, and at the discretion of
                      •   appropriately reflect market conditions and the
                                                                                       the Board. Sales employees are rewarded based on their
                          organisational context
                                                                                       achievement of sales targets.
                      •   recognise and reward high performance
                                                                                       For Executive Team roles, the percentage value of the STIs
                      •   align with creating shareholder value.                       as part of their total remuneration reflects the complexity
                      The Remuneration and Human Resources Committee                   and level of the roles and is set at 30% of their base
                      regularly reviews remuneration policy and practice, with         salaries. For the CE, the percentage value is 40%.
                      the Board approving executive individual performance             LONG-TERM INCENTIVE (LTI). An LTI plan is offered,
                      objectives and company financial performance targets and         at the discretion of the Board, to the New Zealand
                      outcomes on an annual basis.                                     Executive Team to incentivise and reward key executives,
                      Fixed remuneration is benchmarked to market and                  align executives’ and shareholders’ interests, and optimise
                      permanent employees may participate in a short-term              long-term shareholder returns.
                      incentive (STI) scheme at the discretion and invitation of       The percentage value of the LTI is 30% of base salary for
                      the Board. A range of cash and non-cash benefits is offered      executive management, and 40% of base salary for the CE.
                      (for example employee insurance, enhanced parental leave         Vesting of the LTI is contingent on the individual meeting
                      provisions, the ability to purchase additional leave, and        both absolute and relative TSR performance hurdles at the
                      access to purchasing discounts). The Executive Team and          conclusion of a three-year period. Further details of the LTI
                      CE also have the opportunity to participate in a long-term       plan are provided on page 112.
                      incentive (LTI) plan. Both the STI scheme and LTI plan
                      are variable, performance-based incentives and are only          EMPLOYEE SHARE OWNERSHIP. Employees can
                      awarded if specific financial and non-financial performance      also choose to join Meridian’s employee share ownership
                      hurdles are met, and at the discretion of the Board.             plan, MyShare. Under MyShare, employees may

                      FIXED REMUNERATION. Fixed remuneration consists                  between $500 and $5,000 per annum. After three years,

                      a maximum of 4%. Salaries are reviewed annually against          to ongoing employment (Tenure Award Shares) and the
